# 04 マスク付き積和：全方程式（実装の全ステップ）

対象：独立概念モデル（70 評価単位、架空データで検証）の統一系。**J-CAT 原版の式ではない。** この文書は `research_code/tools_equations_export.py` が、エンジンが実行する仕様データ（`specs/`）から機械的に書き起こしたもの。手で書いた式はない。

## 1. 一般式

$$ \mathrm{out}[g] \;=\; \sum_{x \in \mathcal{T}} \; \sum_{t \in \mathcal{E}} \; \prod_{k} \varphi_{t,k}(x) \quad\text{（}g\text{ ごとにまとめる）} $$

- $\mathcal{T}$：表（添字集合）。行 $x$。$\pi(x)$ は子表の行の親行、$x$ 自身は行番号（`_row`）。
- $\mathcal{E}$：項の一覧。項は因子 $\varphi$ の積。
- 因子は次の 3 種類だけ：
  1. 列（数値）：$c[x]$、参照を通した列 $c\big[r[x]\big]$、1 行表の値 $c^{\langle S\rangle}$、定数。
  2. 述語（0/1）：$[c[x] \neq \bot]$（値がある）、$[\ell \le c[x] \le u]$（開閉あり）、$[a = b]$、$[a \le b]$、およびその否定 $\neg[\cdot] = 1-[\cdot]$。
  3. 1 列の関数：$1/a$、$1-a$、$1/(1-a)$、$a/(1-a)$。
- まとめ方：行ごと（$\mathrm{out}[x]$）／合計（$\mathrm{out}$、1 行表 `S` の列）／参照先の行ごと（$\mathrm{out}[y] = \sum_{x: r[x]=y}$）。
- 検索（match）：$\mathrm{ref}[x] = \min\{ y \in \mathcal{U} : \mathrm{on}(y) = \mathrm{keys}(x) \}$（最初に一致した行。なければ $\bot$）。

## 2. マスクと未知値の規則

- **マスク**：述語の積。項の述語がすべて 1 のときだけ、その項が寄与する。述語が 1 つでも 0 なら、値が欠けていても寄与は厳密に 0。
- **未知値** $\bot$：述語がすべて 1 なのに列の値が欠けた項（または関数値が有限でない項）は欠損。欠損の行を含む群は欠損。読み出しは欠損を None にする。欠損を 0 とみなすことはない。
- 述語を欠損値に当てると 0（`present` も含む）。否定は 1。

## 3. 全ステップ

各ステップの末尾の【】は、評価単位（または共通処理）と、展開元のテンプレート。

### 3.1 P1：積和①（T によらない量）（1528 ステップ：積和 1358、検索 170）

#### P1 / A13-05

P1-0021【A13-05】
$$ \mathtt{a05.ok}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{quantity}[x] < \infty] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{waste\_rate}[x] < 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0022【A13-05】
$$ \mathtt{a05.q\_ord}[x] = [\mathtt{a05.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{quantity\_basis}[x] = \text{``DESIGN''}] \cdot \mathtt{quantity}[x] \cdot \frac{1}{1-\mathtt{waste\_rate}[x]} \;+\; [\mathtt{a05.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_basis}[x] = \text{``DESIGN''}] \cdot \mathtt{quantity}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0023【A13-05】
$$ \mathtt{a05.q\_inst}[x] = [\mathtt{a05.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{quantity\_basis}[x] = \text{``DESIGN''}] \cdot \mathtt{quantity}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{a05.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_basis}[x] = \text{``DESIGN''}] \cdot \mathtt{quantity}[x] \cdot (1-\mathtt{waste\_rate}[x]),\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0024【A13-05】
$$ \mathtt{a05.n\_skipped} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big([\mathtt{a05.ok}[x] = 0]\Big) $$


#### P1 / X-13

P1-0025【X-13】
$$ \mathtt{x13.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{a05.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{reuse\_rate}[x] \le 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0026【X-13】
$$ \mathtt{x13.diff}[x] = [\mathtt{x13.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a05.q\_ord}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{x13.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{a05.q\_inst}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0027【X-13】
$$ \mathtt{x13.disposed}[x] = [\mathtt{x13.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{x13.diff}[x] \cdot (1-\mathtt{reuse\_rate}[x]),\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0028【X-13】
$$ \mathtt{x13.net}[x] = [\mathtt{x13.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a05.q\_inst}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{x13.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{x13.disposed}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$


#### P1 / A13-02

P1-0029【A13-02・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{a02.c.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{factors} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{factor\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.composition} $$

P1-0030【A13-02・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{a02.c.unit}[x] = \mathtt{unit}\big[\mathtt{a02.c.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.composition} $$

P1-0031【A13-02・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{a02.c.c.xref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{\_none} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (x, \text{``kg''}, \mathtt{a02.c.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.composition} $$

P1-0032【A13-02・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{a02.c.c.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\text{``kg''}, \mathtt{a02.c.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.composition} $$

P1-0033【A13-02・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{a02.c.c.ok}[x] = [\text{``kg''} = \mathtt{a02.c.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\text{``kg''} = \mathtt{a02.c.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{a02.c.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\text{``kg''} = \mathtt{a02.c.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{a02.c.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{a02.c.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.composition} $$

P1-0034【A13-02・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{a02.c.c.f}[x] = [\text{``kg''} = \mathtt{a02.c.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\text{``kg''} = \mathtt{a02.c.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{a02.c.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{a02.c.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\text{``kg''} = \mathtt{a02.c.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{a02.c.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{a02.c.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{a02.c.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.composition} $$

P1-0035【A13-02・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{a02.c.ok}[x] = [\text{``kg''} \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{a02.c.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.A13.is}\big[\mathtt{a02.c.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{a02.c.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.composition} $$

P1-0036【A13-02】
$$ \mathtt{a02.good}[x] = [\mathtt{a02.c.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{mass\_fraction}[x] < \infty],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.composition} $$

P1-0037【A13-02】
$$ \mathtt{a02.n\_bad}[y] = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials.composition} \,:\, \pi(x) = y} \Big([\mathtt{a02.good}[x] = 0]\Big) $$

P1-0038【A13-02】
$$ \mathtt{a02.sum\_frac}[y] = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials.composition} \,:\, \pi(x) = y} \Big([\mathtt{a02.good}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{mass\_fraction}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0039【A13-02】
$$ \mathtt{a02.value}[y] = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials.composition} \,:\, \pi(x) = y} \Big([\mathtt{a02.good}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{mass\_fraction}[x] \cdot \mathtt{a02.c.c.f}[x] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{a02.c.ref}[x]\big]\Big) $$

P1-0040【A13-02】
$$ \mathtt{a02.resid}[x] = \mathtt{a02.sum\_frac}[x] \;+\; -1,\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0041【A13-02】
$$ \mathtt{a02.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{composition\#}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{a02.n\_bad}[x] = 0] \cdot [-1e-06 \le \mathtt{a02.resid}[x] \le 1e-06],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0042【A13-02】
$$ \mathtt{a02.n\_invalid} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big([\mathtt{composition\#}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{a02.ok}[x] = 0]\Big) $$


#### P1 / X-15

P1-0043【X-15・FACTOR】

P1-0046【X-15・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x15.e.c.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x], \mathtt{x15.e.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0047【X-15・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x15.e.c.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x15.e.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x15.e.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.e.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x15.e.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{x15.e.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.e.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0048【X-15・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x15.e.c.f}[x] = [\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x15.e.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x15.e.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.e.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{x15.e.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x15.e.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{x15.e.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.e.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{x15.e.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0049【X-15・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{x15.e.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{x15.e.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.A13.is}\big[\mathtt{x15.e.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.e.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0050【X-15】
$$ \mathtt{x15.frow}[x] = \mathtt{x15.e.ref}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.epd.declared\_stages} $$

P1-0051【X-15】
$$ \mathtt{x15.srow}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{factors.stages} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{\_pos}[y]) = (\mathtt{x15.frow}[x], \mathtt{\_pos}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.epd.declared\_stages} $$

P1-0052【X-15】
$$ \mathtt{x15.n\_eq}[y] = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials.epd.declared\_stages} \,:\, \pi(x) = y} \Big([\mathtt{x15.srow}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{value}[x] = \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{x15.srow}[x]\big]]\Big) $$

P1-0053【X-15】
$$ \mathtt{x15.is\_epd}[x] = [\mathtt{epd\#}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.e.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{epd.declared\_stages\#}[x] = \mathtt{stages\#}\big[\mathtt{x15.e.ref}[x]\big]] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.n\_eq}[x] = \mathtt{epd.declared\_stages\#}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{epd.declared\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x15.e.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{epd.valid\_until}[x] = \mathtt{valid\_until}\big[\mathtt{x15.e.ref}[x]\big]] \cdot [\mathtt{assessment\_date}^{\langle \mathtt{project}\rangle} \le \mathtt{valid\_until}\big[\mathtt{x15.e.ref}[x]\big]],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0054【X-15・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x15.k.xref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{materials.conversions} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (x, \mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x], \text{``kg''}) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0055【X-15・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x15.k.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x], \text{``kg''}) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0056【X-15・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x15.k.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \text{``kg''}] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \text{``kg''}] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.k.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \text{``kg''}] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{x15.k.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.k.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0057【X-15・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x15.k.f}[x] = [\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \text{``kg''}] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \text{``kg''}] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.k.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{x15.k.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \text{``kg''}] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{x15.k.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.k.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{x15.k.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0058【X-15】
$$ \mathtt{x15.is\_comp}[x] = [\mathtt{x15.is\_epd}[x] = 0] \cdot [\mathtt{composition\#}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{a02.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.k.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0059【X-15・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{x15.g.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{factors} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{factor\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0060【X-15・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{x15.g.unit}[x] = \mathtt{unit}\big[\mathtt{x15.g.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0061【X-15・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x15.g.c.xref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{materials.conversions} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (x, \mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x], \mathtt{x15.g.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0062【X-15・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x15.g.c.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x], \mathtt{x15.g.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0063【X-15・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x15.g.c.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x15.g.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x15.g.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.g.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x15.g.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{x15.g.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.g.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0064【X-15・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x15.g.c.f}[x] = [\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x15.g.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x15.g.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.g.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{x15.g.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x15.g.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{x15.g.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.g.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{x15.g.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0065【X-15・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{x15.g.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{x15.g.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.A13.is}\big[\mathtt{x15.g.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.g.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0066【X-15】
$$ \mathtt{x15.is\_gen}[x] = [\mathtt{x15.is\_epd}[x] = 0] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{composition\#}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.g.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0067【X-15】
$$ \mathtt{x15.resolved}[x] = [\mathtt{x15.is\_epd}[x] = 1] \;+\; [\mathtt{x15.is\_comp}[x] = 1] \;+\; [\mathtt{x15.is\_gen}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0068【X-15】
$$ \mathtt{x15.factor}[x] = [\mathtt{x15.is\_epd}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{x15.e.ref}[x]\big] \;+\; [\mathtt{x15.is\_comp}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a02.value}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{x15.is\_gen}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{x15.g.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0069【X-15】
$$ \mathtt{x15.conv}[x] = [\mathtt{x15.is\_epd}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{x15.e.c.f}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{x15.is\_comp}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{x15.k.f}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{x15.is\_gen}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{x15.g.c.f}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0070【X-15】
$$ \mathtt{x15.n\_unresolved} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big([\mathtt{x15.resolved}[x] = 0]\Big) $$

P1-0071【X-15】
$$ \mathtt{x15.n\_epd\_declined} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big([\mathtt{epd\#}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.is\_epd}[x] = 0]\Big) $$


#### P1 / A13-01

P1-0072【A13-01】
$$ \mathtt{a01.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{x15.resolved}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.is\_epd}[x] = 0] \cdot [\mathtt{a05.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0073【A13-01】
$$ \mathtt{a01.by\_item}[x] = [\mathtt{a01.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a05.q\_ord}[x] \cdot \mathtt{x15.conv}[x] \cdot \mathtt{x15.factor}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0074【A13-01】
$$ \mathtt{a01.value} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big(\mathtt{a01.by\_item}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0075【A13-01】
$$ \mathtt{a01.n\_skipped} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big([\mathtt{x15.resolved}[x] = 0] \;+\; [\mathtt{x15.resolved}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.is\_epd}[x] = 0] \cdot [\mathtt{a05.ok}[x] = 0]\Big) $$


#### P1 / A13-03

P1-0076【A13-03】
$$ \mathtt{a03.by\_item}[x] = [\mathtt{x15.is\_epd}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{a05.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a05.q\_ord}[x] \cdot \mathtt{x15.conv}[x] \cdot \mathtt{x15.factor}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0077【A13-03】
$$ \mathtt{a03.value} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big(\mathtt{a03.by\_item}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0078【A13-03】
$$ \mathtt{a03.n\_skipped} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big([\mathtt{x15.is\_epd}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{a05.ok}[x] = 0]\Big) $$


#### P1 / A13-04

P1-0079【A13-04】
$$ \mathtt{a04.in}[x] = [0 < \mathtt{unquantified\_cost\_ratio}[x] < 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0080【A13-04】
$$ \mathtt{a04.base}[x] = \mathtt{a01.by\_item}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{a03.by\_item}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0081【A13-04】
$$ \mathtt{a04.by\_item}[x] = [\mathtt{a04.in}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a04.base}[x] \cdot \frac{\mathtt{unquantified\_cost\_ratio}[x]}{1-\mathtt{unquantified\_cost\_ratio}[x]},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0082【A13-04】
$$ \mathtt{a04.value} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big(\mathtt{a04.by\_item}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0083【A13-04】
$$ \mathtt{a04.n\_skipped} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big([\mathtt{unquantified\_cost\_ratio}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{unquantified\_cost\_ratio}[x] = 0] \cdot [\mathtt{a04.in}[x] = 0]\Big) $$


#### P1 / X-01

P1-0084【X-01・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{x01.f.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{factors} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{factor\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0085【X-01・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{x01.f.unit}[x] = \mathtt{unit}\big[\mathtt{x01.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0086【X-01・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x01.f.c.xref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{\_none} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (x, \mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x], \mathtt{x01.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0087【X-01・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x01.f.c.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x], \mathtt{x01.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0088【X-01・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x01.f.c.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x01.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x01.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{x01.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x01.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{x01.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{x01.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0089【X-01・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x01.f.c.f}[x] = [\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x01.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x01.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{x01.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{x01.f.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{x01.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{x01.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{x01.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{x01.f.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0090【X-01・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{x01.f.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{x01.f.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.A13.is}\big[\mathtt{x01.f.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{x01.f.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0091【X-01】
$$ \mathtt{x01.ok}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{quantity}[x] < \infty] \cdot [\mathtt{x01.f.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0092【X-01】
$$ \mathtt{x01.by\_item}[x] = [\mathtt{x01.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{quantity}[x] \cdot \mathtt{x01.f.c.f}[x] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{x01.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0093【X-01】
$$ \mathtt{x01.value} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{equipment}} \Big(\mathtt{x01.by\_item}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0094【X-01】
$$ \mathtt{x01.n\_skipped} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{equipment}} \Big([\mathtt{x01.ok}[x] = 0]\Big) $$


#### P1 / X-03

P1-0095【X-03】
$$ \mathtt{x03.act}[x] = [\mathtt{x15.is\_comp}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{a05.ok}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.composition} $$

P1-0096【X-03】
$$ \mathtt{x03.m}[x] = [\mathtt{x03.act}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a05.q\_ord}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big] \cdot \mathtt{x15.conv}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big] \cdot \mathtt{mass\_fraction}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.composition} $$

P1-0097【X-03】
$$ \mathtt{x03.e}[x] = [\mathtt{x03.act}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{x03.m}[x] \cdot \mathtt{a02.c.c.f}[x] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{a02.c.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.composition} $$

P1-0098【X-03】
$$ \mathtt{x03.value} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials.composition}} \Big(\mathtt{x03.e}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0099【X-03】
$$ \mathtt{x03.mass}[y] = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials.composition} \,:\, \mathtt{a02.c.ref}[x] = y} \Big([\mathtt{x03.act}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{x03.m}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0100【X-03】
$$ \mathtt{x03.emission}[y] = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials.composition} \,:\, \mathtt{a02.c.ref}[x] = y} \Big([\mathtt{x03.act}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{x03.e}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0101【X-03】
$$ \mathtt{x03.n}[y] = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials.composition} \,:\, \mathtt{a02.c.ref}[x] = y} \Big([\mathtt{x03.act}[x] = 1]\Big) $$


#### P1 / A4-01

P1-0102【A4-01・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{a41.f.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{factors} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{transport.factor\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0104【A4-01・FACTOR/CONV】

P1-0106【A4-01・FACTOR/CONV】

P1-0107【A4-01・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{a41.f.c.f}[x] = [\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{a41.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{a41.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{a41.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{a41.f.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{a41.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{a41.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{a41.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{a41.f.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0108【A4-01・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{a41.f.ok}[x] = [\text{``tkm''} \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{a41.f.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.A4.is}\big[\mathtt{a41.f.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{a41.f.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0109【A4-01】
$$ \mathtt{a41.ok}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{transport.delivery\_factor\_id}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{a41.f.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{transport.mass\_t}[x] < \infty] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{transport.distance\_km}[x] < \infty],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0110【A4-01】
$$ \mathtt{a41.by\_item}[x] = [\mathtt{a41.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{transport.mass\_t}[x] \cdot \mathtt{transport.distance\_km}[x] \cdot \mathtt{a41.f.c.f}[x] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{a41.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0111【A4-01】
$$ \mathtt{a41.value} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big(\mathtt{a41.by\_item}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0112【A4-01】
$$ \mathtt{a41.n\_skipped} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big(\neg[\mathtt{transport.delivery\_factor\_id}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{a41.ok}[x] = 0]\Big) $$

$$ \mathtt{x12.f.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{factors} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{transport.factor\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0116【X-12・FACTOR/CONV】

P1-0118【X-12・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x12.f.c.f}[x] = [\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{x12.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{x12.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{x12.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{x12.f.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{x12.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{x12.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{x12.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{x12.f.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0119【X-12・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{x12.f.ok}[x] = [\text{``tkm''} \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{x12.f.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.A4.is}\big[\mathtt{x12.f.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{x12.f.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0120【X-12】
$$ \mathtt{x12.ok}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{transport.delivery\_factor\_id}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{x12.f.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{transport.mass\_t}[x] < \infty] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{transport.distance\_km}[x] < \infty] \cdot [0 < \mathtt{transport.load\_factor}[x] \le \infty],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0121【X-12】
$$ \mathtt{x12.by\_item}[x] = [\mathtt{x12.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{transport.mass\_t}[x] \cdot \mathtt{transport.distance\_km}[x] \cdot \mathtt{x12.f.c.f}[x] \cdot \frac{1}{\mathtt{transport.load\_factor}[x]} \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{x12.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0122【X-12】

P1-0123【X-12】

P1-0129【A4-02・FACTOR/CONV】

P1-0130【A4-02・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{a42.f.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{a42.f.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.A4.is}\big[\mathtt{a42.f.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{a42.f.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0131【A4-02】

P1-0132【A4-02】

P1-0133【A4-02】

P1-0134【A4-02】

#### P1 / A5-01

P1-0135【A5-01】
$$ \mathtt{a51.gu\_ok}[x] = [\mathtt{grid.unit\~{}h}[x] = \text{``kgCO2e''}] \cdot [\mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x] = \text{``''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0136【A5-01・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{a51.c.xref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{\_none} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (x, \text{``kWh''}, \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0137【A5-01・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{a51.c.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\text{``kWh''}, \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0138【A5-01・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{a51.c.ok}[x] = [\text{``kWh''} = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\text{``kWh''} = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{a51.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\text{``kWh''} = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{a51.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{a51.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0139【A5-01・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{a51.c.f}[x] = [\text{``kWh''} = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\text{``kWh''} = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{a51.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{a51.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\text{``kWh''} = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{a51.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{a51.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{a51.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0140【A5-01】
$$ \mathtt{a51.ok}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{electricity\_kwh}^{\langle \mathtt{construction}\rangle} < \infty] \cdot [\mathtt{a51.gu\_ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{a51.c.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{grid.licence\_status}[x] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0141【A5-01】
$$ \mathtt{a51.value} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{operation}} \Big([\mathtt{a51.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{electricity\_kwh}^{\langle \mathtt{construction}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{a51.c.f}[x] \cdot \mathtt{grid.construction\_value}[x]\Big) $$


#### P1 / A5-02

P1-0142【A5-02・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{a52.f.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{factors} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{factor\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{construction.fuels} $$

P1-0143【A5-02・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{a52.f.unit}[x] = \mathtt{unit}\big[\mathtt{a52.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{construction.fuels} $$

P1-0144【A5-02・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{a52.f.c.xref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{construction.fuels.conversions} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (x, \mathtt{unit}[x], \mathtt{a52.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{construction.fuels} $$

P1-0145【A5-02・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{a52.f.c.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\mathtt{unit}[x], \mathtt{a52.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{construction.fuels} $$

P1-0146【A5-02・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{a52.f.c.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{unit}[x] = \mathtt{a52.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{unit}[x] = \mathtt{a52.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{a52.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{unit}[x] = \mathtt{a52.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{a52.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{a52.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{construction.fuels} $$

P1-0147【A5-02・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{a52.f.c.f}[x] = [\mathtt{unit}[x] = \mathtt{a52.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{unit}[x] = \mathtt{a52.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{a52.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{a52.f.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{unit}[x] = \mathtt{a52.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{a52.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{a52.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{a52.f.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{construction.fuels} $$

P1-0148【A5-02・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{a52.f.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{unit}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{a52.f.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.A5.is}\big[\mathtt{a52.f.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{a52.f.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{construction.fuels} $$

P1-0149【A5-02】
$$ \mathtt{a52.ok}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{quantity}[x] < \infty] \cdot [\mathtt{a52.f.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{construction.fuels} $$

P1-0150【A5-02】
$$ \mathtt{a52.value} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{construction.fuels}} \Big([\mathtt{a52.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{quantity}[x] \cdot \mathtt{a52.f.c.f}[x] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{a52.f.ref}[x]\big]\Big) $$

P1-0151【A5-02】
$$ \mathtt{a52.n\_skipped} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{construction.fuels}} \Big([\mathtt{a52.ok}[x] = 0]\Big) $$


#### P1 / A5-03

P1-0152【A5-03・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{a53.f.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{factors} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{factor\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{construction.waste\_streams} $$

P1-0153【A5-03・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{a53.f.unit}[x] = \mathtt{unit}\big[\mathtt{a53.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{construction.waste\_streams} $$

P1-0154【A5-03・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{a53.f.c.xref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{\_none} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (x, \text{``kg''}, \mathtt{a53.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{construction.waste\_streams} $$

P1-0155【A5-03・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{a53.f.c.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\text{``kg''}, \mathtt{a53.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{construction.waste\_streams} $$

P1-0156【A5-03・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{a53.f.c.ok}[x] = [\text{``kg''} = \mathtt{a53.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\text{``kg''} = \mathtt{a53.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{a53.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\text{``kg''} = \mathtt{a53.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{a53.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{a53.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{construction.waste\_streams} $$

P1-0157【A5-03・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{a53.f.c.f}[x] = [\text{``kg''} = \mathtt{a53.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\text{``kg''} = \mathtt{a53.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{a53.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{a53.f.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\text{``kg''} = \mathtt{a53.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{a53.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{a53.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{a53.f.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{construction.waste\_streams} $$

P1-0158【A5-03・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{a53.f.ok}[x] = [\text{``kg''} \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{a53.f.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.A5.is}\big[\mathtt{a53.f.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{a53.f.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{construction.waste\_streams} $$

P1-0159【A5-03】
$$ \mathtt{a53.ok}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{mass\_kg}[x] < \infty] \cdot [\mathtt{a53.f.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{construction.waste\_streams} $$

P1-0160【A5-03】
$$ \mathtt{a53.value} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{construction.waste\_streams}} \Big([\mathtt{a53.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{mass\_kg}[x] \cdot \mathtt{a53.f.c.f}[x] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{a53.f.ref}[x]\big]\Big) $$

P1-0161【A5-03】
$$ \mathtt{a53.n\_skipped} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{construction.waste\_streams}} \Big([\mathtt{a53.ok}[x] = 0]\Big) $$


#### P1 / A5-04

P1-0162【A5-04】
$$ \mathtt{a54.value}[x] = \mathtt{a51.value}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{a52.value}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{a53.value}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$


#### P1 / X-17

P1-0163【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.REBAR.in}[x] = [\mathtt{material\_class}[x] = \text{``REBAR''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0164【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.REBAR.sum} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big([\mathtt{x17.REBAR.in}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a01.by\_item}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{x17.REBAR.in}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a03.by\_item}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0165【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.REBAR.fbar}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{reinforcement\_kg}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle} = 0] \cdot \mathtt{x17.REBAR.sum}[x] \cdot \frac{1}{\mathtt{reinforcement\_kg}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle}},\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0166【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.REBAR.e}[x] = \mathtt{reinforcement\_kg}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{x17.REBAR.fbar}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0167【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.STRUCTURAL\_STEEL.in}[x] = [\mathtt{material\_class}[x] = \text{``STRUCTURAL\_STEEL''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0168【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.STRUCTURAL\_STEEL.sum} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big([\mathtt{x17.STRUCTURAL\_STEEL.in}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a01.by\_item}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{x17.STRUCTURAL\_STEEL.in}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a03.by\_item}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0169【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.STRUCTURAL\_STEEL.fbar}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{steel\_kg}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle} = 0] \cdot \mathtt{x17.STRUCTURAL\_STEEL.sum}[x] \cdot \frac{1}{\mathtt{steel\_kg}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle}},\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0170【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.STRUCTURAL\_STEEL.e}[x] = \mathtt{steel\_kg}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{x17.STRUCTURAL\_STEEL.fbar}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0171【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.CONCRETE.in}[x] = [\mathtt{material\_class}[x] = \text{``CONCRETE''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0172【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.CONCRETE.sum} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big([\mathtt{x17.CONCRETE.in}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a01.by\_item}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{x17.CONCRETE.in}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a03.by\_item}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0173【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.CONCRETE.fbar}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{concrete\_m3}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle} = 0] \cdot \mathtt{x17.CONCRETE.sum}[x] \cdot \frac{1}{\mathtt{concrete\_m3}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle}},\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0174【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.CONCRETE.e}[x] = \mathtt{concrete\_m3}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{x17.CONCRETE.fbar}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0175【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.TIMBER.in}[x] = [\mathtt{material\_class}[x] = \text{``TIMBER''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0176【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.TIMBER.sum} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big([\mathtt{x17.TIMBER.in}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a01.by\_item}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{x17.TIMBER.in}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a03.by\_item}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0177【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.TIMBER.fbar}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{timber\_m3}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle} = 0] \cdot \mathtt{x17.TIMBER.sum}[x] \cdot \frac{1}{\mathtt{timber\_m3}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle}},\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0178【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.TIMBER.e}[x] = \mathtt{timber\_m3}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{x17.TIMBER.fbar}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0179【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.ENVELOPE.in}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{material\_class}[x] = \text{``REBAR''}] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{material\_class}[x] = \text{``STRUCTURAL\_STEEL''}] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{material\_class}[x] = \text{``CONCRETE''}] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{material\_class}[x] = \text{``TIMBER''}] \cdot [\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \text{``m2''}] \cdot [\mathtt{construction\_division}[x] = \text{``ROOF\_EXTERIOR\_WALL''}] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{material\_class}[x] = \text{``REBAR''}] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{material\_class}[x] = \text{``STRUCTURAL\_STEEL''}] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{material\_class}[x] = \text{``CONCRETE''}] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{material\_class}[x] = \text{``TIMBER''}] \cdot [\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \text{``m2''}] \cdot [\mathtt{construction\_division}[x] = \text{``OPENINGS''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0180【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.ENVELOPE.sum} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big([\mathtt{x17.ENVELOPE.in}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a01.by\_item}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{x17.ENVELOPE.in}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a03.by\_item}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0181【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.ENVELOPE.fbar}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{envelope\_m2}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle} = 0] \cdot \mathtt{x17.ENVELOPE.sum}[x] \cdot \frac{1}{\mathtt{envelope\_m2}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle}},\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0182【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.ENVELOPE.e}[x] = \mathtt{envelope\_m2}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{x17.ENVELOPE.fbar}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0183【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.INTERIOR.in}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{material\_class}[x] = \text{``REBAR''}] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{material\_class}[x] = \text{``STRUCTURAL\_STEEL''}] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{material\_class}[x] = \text{``CONCRETE''}] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{material\_class}[x] = \text{``TIMBER''}] \cdot [\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \text{``m2''}] \cdot [\mathtt{construction\_division}[x] = \text{``INTERIOR''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0184【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.INTERIOR.sum} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big([\mathtt{x17.INTERIOR.in}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a01.by\_item}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{x17.INTERIOR.in}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{a03.by\_item}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0185【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.INTERIOR.fbar}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{interior\_m2}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle} = 0] \cdot \mathtt{x17.INTERIOR.sum}[x] \cdot \frac{1}{\mathtt{interior\_m2}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle}},\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0186【X-17・CLASS】
$$ \mathtt{x17.INTERIOR.e}[x] = \mathtt{interior\_m2}^{\langle \mathtt{summary\_quantities}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{x17.INTERIOR.fbar}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0187【X-17】
$$ \mathtt{x17.value}[x] = \mathtt{x17.REBAR.e}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{x17.STRUCTURAL\_STEEL.e}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{x17.CONCRETE.e}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{x17.TIMBER.e}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{x17.ENVELOPE.e}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{x17.INTERIOR.e}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$


#### P1 / X-16

P1-0188【X-16】
$$ \mathtt{x16.pc}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{price\_index} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{price\_year}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{cost\_items} $$

P1-0189【X-16】
$$ \mathtt{x16.pb}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{price\_index} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{factor\_base\_year}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{cost\_items} $$

P1-0190【X-16・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{x16.f.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{factors} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{factor\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{cost\_items} $$

P1-0191【X-16・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{x16.f.unit}[x] = \mathtt{unit}\big[\mathtt{x16.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{cost\_items} $$

P1-0192【X-16・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x16.f.c.xref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{\_none} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (x, \mathtt{currency}[x], \mathtt{x16.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{cost\_items} $$

P1-0193【X-16・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x16.f.c.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\mathtt{currency}[x], \mathtt{x16.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{cost\_items} $$

P1-0194【X-16・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x16.f.c.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{currency}[x] = \mathtt{x16.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{currency}[x] = \mathtt{x16.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{x16.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{currency}[x] = \mathtt{x16.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{x16.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{x16.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{cost\_items} $$

P1-0195【X-16・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x16.f.c.f}[x] = [\mathtt{currency}[x] = \mathtt{x16.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{currency}[x] = \mathtt{x16.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{x16.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{x16.f.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{currency}[x] = \mathtt{x16.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{x16.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{x16.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{x16.f.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{cost\_items} $$

P1-0196【X-16・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{x16.f.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{currency}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{x16.f.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.A13.is}\big[\mathtt{x16.f.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{x16.f.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{cost\_items} $$

P1-0197【X-16】
$$ \mathtt{x16.ok}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{amount}[x] < \infty] \cdot [\mathtt{x16.f.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [0 < \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{x16.pc}[x]\big] \le \infty] \cdot [0 < \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{x16.pb}[x]\big] \le \infty],\quad x \in \mathtt{cost\_items} $$

P1-0198【X-16】
$$ \mathtt{x16.by\_item}[x] = [\mathtt{x16.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{amount}[x] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{x16.pb}[x]\big] \cdot \frac{1}{\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{x16.pc}[x]\big]} \cdot \mathtt{x16.f.c.f}[x] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{x16.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{cost\_items} $$

P1-0199【X-16】
$$ \mathtt{x16.value} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{cost\_items}} \Big(\mathtt{x16.by\_item}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0200【X-16】
$$ \mathtt{x16.n\_skipped} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{cost\_items}} \Big([\mathtt{x16.ok}[x] = 0]\Big) $$


#### P1 / C1-01

P1-0201【C1-01・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{c11.f.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{factors} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{factor\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.demolition\_activities} $$

P1-0202【C1-01・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{c11.f.unit}[x] = \mathtt{unit}\big[\mathtt{c11.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.demolition\_activities} $$

P1-0203【C1-01・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{c11.f.c.xref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{\_none} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (x, \mathtt{unit}[x], \mathtt{c11.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.demolition\_activities} $$

P1-0204【C1-01・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{c11.f.c.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\mathtt{unit}[x], \mathtt{c11.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.demolition\_activities} $$

P1-0205【C1-01・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{c11.f.c.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{unit}[x] = \mathtt{c11.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{unit}[x] = \mathtt{c11.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{c11.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{unit}[x] = \mathtt{c11.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{c11.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{c11.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.demolition\_activities} $$

P1-0206【C1-01・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{c11.f.c.f}[x] = [\mathtt{unit}[x] = \mathtt{c11.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{unit}[x] = \mathtt{c11.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{c11.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{c11.f.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{unit}[x] = \mathtt{c11.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{c11.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{c11.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{c11.f.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.demolition\_activities} $$

P1-0207【C1-01・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{c11.f.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{unit}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{c11.f.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.C1.is}\big[\mathtt{c11.f.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{c11.f.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.demolition\_activities} $$

P1-0208【C1-01】
$$ \mathtt{c11.ok}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{quantity}[x] < \infty] \cdot [\mathtt{c11.f.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.demolition\_activities} $$

P1-0209【C1-01】
$$ \mathtt{c11.value} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.demolition\_activities}} \Big([\mathtt{c11.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{quantity}[x] \cdot \mathtt{c11.f.c.f}[x] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{c11.f.ref}[x]\big]\Big) $$

P1-0210【C1-01】
$$ \mathtt{c11.n\_skipped} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.demolition\_activities}} \Big([\mathtt{c11.ok}[x] = 0]\Big) $$


#### P1 / C2-01

P1-0211【C2-01・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{c21.f.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{factors} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{end\_of\_life.haul\_factor\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0212【C2-01・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{c21.f.unit}[x] = \mathtt{unit}\big[\mathtt{c21.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0213【C2-01・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{c21.f.c.xref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{\_none} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (x, \text{``tkm''}, \mathtt{c21.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0214【C2-01・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{c21.f.c.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\text{``tkm''}, \mathtt{c21.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0215【C2-01・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{c21.f.c.ok}[x] = [\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{c21.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{c21.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{c21.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{c21.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{c21.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{c21.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0216【C2-01・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{c21.f.c.f}[x] = [\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{c21.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{c21.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{c21.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{c21.f.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{c21.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{c21.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{c21.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{c21.f.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0217【C2-01・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{c21.f.ok}[x] = [\text{``tkm''} \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{c21.f.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.C2.is}\big[\mathtt{c21.f.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{c21.f.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0218【C2-01】
$$ \mathtt{c21.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{c21.f.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{end\_of\_life.mass\_kg}[x] < \infty] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{end\_of\_life.haul\_distance\_km}[x] < \infty],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0219【C2-01】
$$ \mathtt{c21.by\_item}[x] = [\mathtt{c21.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{end\_of\_life.mass\_kg}[x] \cdot 0.001 \cdot \mathtt{end\_of\_life.haul\_distance\_km}[x] \cdot \mathtt{c21.f.c.f}[x] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{c21.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0220【C2-01】
$$ \mathtt{c21.mat} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big(\mathtt{c21.by\_item}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0221【C2-01】
$$ \mathtt{c21.nm} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big([\mathtt{c21.ok}[x] = 0]\Big) $$

P1-0222【C2-01・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{c21.h.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{factors} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{factor\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.waste\_hauls} $$

P1-0223【C2-01・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{c21.h.unit}[x] = \mathtt{unit}\big[\mathtt{c21.h.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.waste\_hauls} $$

P1-0224【C2-01・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{c21.h.c.xref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{\_none} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (x, \text{``tkm''}, \mathtt{c21.h.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.waste\_hauls} $$

P1-0225【C2-01・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{c21.h.c.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\text{``tkm''}, \mathtt{c21.h.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.waste\_hauls} $$

P1-0226【C2-01・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{c21.h.c.ok}[x] = [\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{c21.h.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{c21.h.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{c21.h.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{c21.h.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{c21.h.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{c21.h.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.waste\_hauls} $$

P1-0227【C2-01・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{c21.h.c.f}[x] = [\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{c21.h.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{c21.h.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{c21.h.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{c21.h.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\text{``tkm''} = \mathtt{c21.h.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{c21.h.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{c21.h.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{c21.h.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.waste\_hauls} $$

P1-0228【C2-01・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{c21.h.ok}[x] = [\text{``tkm''} \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{c21.h.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.C2.is}\big[\mathtt{c21.h.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{c21.h.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.waste\_hauls} $$

P1-0229【C2-01】
$$ \mathtt{c21.hok}[x] = [\mathtt{c21.h.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{mass\_t}[x] < \infty] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{distance\_km}[x] < \infty],\quad x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.waste\_hauls} $$

P1-0230【C2-01】
$$ \mathtt{c21.haul} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.waste\_hauls}} \Big([\mathtt{c21.hok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{mass\_t}[x] \cdot \mathtt{distance\_km}[x] \cdot \mathtt{c21.h.c.f}[x] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{c21.h.ref}[x]\big]\Big) $$

P1-0231【C2-01】
$$ \mathtt{c21.nh} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{end\_of\_life.waste\_hauls}} \Big([\mathtt{c21.hok}[x] = 0]\Big) $$

P1-0232【C2-01】
$$ \mathtt{c21.value}[x] = \mathtt{c21.mat}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{c21.haul}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0233【C2-01】
$$ \mathtt{c21.n\_skipped}[x] = \mathtt{c21.nm}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{c21.nh}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0240【C3-01・FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{c31.f.ok}[x] = [\text{``kg''} \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{c31.f.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.C3.is}\big[\mathtt{c31.f.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{c31.f.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.end\_of\_life.routes} $$

P1-0241【C3-01】
$$ \mathtt{c31.ok}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{route}[x] = \text{``LANDFILL''}] \cdot [\mathtt{c31.f.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{fraction}[x] < \infty] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{end\_of\_life.mass\_kg}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big] < \infty],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.end\_of\_life.routes} $$

P1-0242【C3-01】
$$ \mathtt{c31.by\_item}[y] = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials.end\_of\_life.routes} \,:\, \pi(x) = y} \Big([\mathtt{c31.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{end\_of\_life.mass\_kg}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big] \cdot \mathtt{fraction}[x] \cdot \mathtt{c31.f.c.f}[x] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{c31.f.ref}[x]\big]\Big) $$

P1-0243【C3-01】
$$ \mathtt{c31.value} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big(\mathtt{c31.by\_item}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0244【C3-01】
$$ \mathtt{c31.rsk}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{route}[x] = \text{``LANDFILL''}] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{end\_of\_life.mass\_kg}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big] < \infty] \cdot [\mathtt{c31.f.ok}[x] = 0] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{route}[x] = \text{``LANDFILL''}] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{end\_of\_life.mass\_kg}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big] < \infty] \cdot [\mathtt{c31.f.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \neg[0 \le \mathtt{fraction}[x] < \infty],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.end\_of\_life.routes} $$

P1-0245【C3-01】
$$ \mathtt{c31.npart}[y] = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials.end\_of\_life.routes} \,:\, \pi(x) = y} \Big(\mathtt{c31.rsk}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0246【C3-01】
$$ \mathtt{c31.nrsk} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials.end\_of\_life.routes}} \Big(\mathtt{c31.rsk}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0247【C3-01】
$$ \mathtt{c31.nsk} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big(\neg[0 \le \mathtt{end\_of\_life.mass\_kg}[x] < \infty]\Big) $$

$$ \mathtt{x14.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{x14.f.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{end\_of\_life\_mass\_kg}[x] < \infty],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0270【X-14】
$$ \mathtt{x14.by\_item}[x] = [\mathtt{x14.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{end\_of\_life\_mass\_kg}[x] \cdot \mathtt{x14.f.c.f}[x] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{x14.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0271【X-14】
$$ \mathtt{x14.value} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{equipment}} \Big(\mathtt{x14.by\_item}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0272【X-14】
$$ \mathtt{x14.n\_skipped} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{equipment}} \Big([\mathtt{x14.ok}[x] = 0]\Big) $$

P1-0297【AMPLITUDES・FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b4.ins.f.c.f}[x] = [\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{b4.ins.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{b4.ins.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{b4.ins.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{b4.ins.f.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{quantity\_unit}[x] = \mathtt{b4.ins.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{b4.ins.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b4.ins.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{b4.ins.f.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0298【AMPLITUDES・FACTOR】

P1-0299【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b4.ins.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{installation\_factor\_id}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b4.ins.f.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{quantity}[x] < \infty],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0300【AMPLITUDES】

P1-0301【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b4.u\_eq}[x] = \mathtt{x01.by\_item}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{b4.dlv}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{b4.ins}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{x14.by\_item}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0302【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{np.a01}[x] = [\mathtt{x15.resolved}[x] = 0] \;+\; [\mathtt{x15.resolved}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{x15.is\_epd}[x] = 0] \cdot [\mathtt{a05.ok}[x] = 0],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0303【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{np.a03}[x] = [\mathtt{x15.is\_epd}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{a05.ok}[x] = 0],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0304【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{np.a41}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{transport.delivery\_factor\_id}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{a41.ok}[x] = 0],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0305【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{np.a42}[x] = [\mathtt{transport.delivery\_factor\_id}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{a42.ok}[x] = 0],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0306【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{np.cnt}[x] = \mathtt{np.a01}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{np.a03}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{np.a41}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{np.a42}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{c21.ok}[x] = 0] \;+\; \neg[0 \le \mathtt{end\_of\_life.mass\_kg}[x] < \infty] \;+\; \mathtt{c31.npart}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{c41.npart}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{installation\_factor\_id}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b4.inst.ok}[x] = 0],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0307【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{np.m}[x] = [1 \le \mathtt{np.cnt}[x] \le \infty],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0308【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{np.cnt\_e}[x] = [\mathtt{x01.ok}[x] = 0] \;+\; [\mathtt{x14.ok}[x] = 0] \;+\; [\mathtt{delivery\_factor\_id}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b4.dlv.ok}[x] = 0] \;+\; [\mathtt{installation\_factor\_id}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b4.ins.ok}[x] = 0],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0309【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{np.e}[x] = [1 \le \mathtt{np.cnt\_e}[x] \le \infty],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0310【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b3.a\_mat}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{repair\_rate}[x] < \infty] \cdot \mathtt{a01.by\_item}[x] \cdot \mathtt{repair\_rate}[x] \;+\; [0 \le \mathtt{repair\_rate}[x] < \infty] \cdot \mathtt{a03.by\_item}[x] \cdot \mathtt{repair\_rate}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0311【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b3.a\_eq}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{repair\_rate}[x] < \infty] \cdot \mathtt{x01.by\_item}[x] \cdot \mathtt{repair\_rate}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0312【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b3.A\_m} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big(\mathtt{b3.a\_mat}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0313【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b3.A\_e} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{equipment}} \Big(\mathtt{b3.a\_eq}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0314【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b3.nsm} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big(\neg[0 \le \mathtt{repair\_rate}[x] < \infty]\Big) $$

P1-0315【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b3.nse} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{equipment}} \Big(\neg[0 \le \mathtt{repair\_rate}[x] < \infty]\Big) $$

P1-0316【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b3.n\_skipped}[x] = \mathtt{b3.nsm}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{b3.nse}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0317【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b3.e0}[x] = \mathtt{a01.by\_item}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{a03.by\_item}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0318【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b3.lines}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{repair\_rate}[x] < \infty] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{repair\_rate}[x] = 0] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{b3.e0}[x] = 0],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0319【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b3.lines\_e}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{repair\_rate}[x] < \infty] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{repair\_rate}[x] = 0] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{x01.by\_item}[x] = 0],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0320【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b5.base}[x] = \mathtt{a01.value}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{a03.value}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{a04.value}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{x01.value}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{a41.value}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{a42.value}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{a54.value}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{c11.value}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{c21.value}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{c31.value}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{c41.value}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{x14.value}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0321【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b5.none}[x] = [\mathtt{reconstruction\_scenario}^{\langle \mathtt{boundary}\rangle} = \text{``NONE''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0322【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b5.live}[x] = [\mathtt{b5.none}[x] = 0] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{reconstruction\_scope\_fraction}^{\langle \mathtt{boundary}\rangle} \le 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0323【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b5.computed}[x] = [\mathtt{b5.none}[x] = 1] \;+\; [\mathtt{b5.live}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0324【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b5.u}[x] = [\mathtt{b5.live}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{reconstruction\_scope\_fraction}^{\langle \mathtt{boundary}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{b5.base}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

P1-0325【AMPLITUDES】
$$ \mathtt{b5.scope}[x] = [\mathtt{b5.live}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{reconstruction\_scope\_fraction}^{\langle \mathtt{boundary}\rangle},\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$


#### P1 / EVENTS

P1-0326【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{me.cal}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} \;:\; (\mathtt{\_pos}[y]) = (\mathtt{cell}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.events} $$

P1-0327【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{me.f}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{materials.events} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{cell}[y]) = (\pi(x), \mathtt{cell}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.events} $$

P1-0328【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{me.first}[x] = [\mathtt{me.f}[x] = x],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.events} $$

P1-0329【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{me.b4}[x] = \mathtt{b4.u\_mat}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.events} $$

P1-0330【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{me.b3}[x] = [\mathtt{me.first}[x] = 1] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{b3.a\_mat}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.events} $$

P1-0331【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{me.np}[x] = [\mathtt{ord}[x] = 0] \cdot \mathtt{np.m}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.events} $$

P1-0332【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{ee.act}[x] = [\mathtt{src}[x] = \text{``LIST''}] \cdot [\mathtt{replacement\_event\_list\#}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \;+\; [\mathtt{src}[x] = \text{``CYCLE''}] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{replacement\_event\_list\#}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment.events} $$

P1-0333【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{ee.cal}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} \;:\; (\mathtt{\_pos}[y]) = (\mathtt{cell}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment.events} $$

P1-0334【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{ee.f}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{equipment.events} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{cell}[y], \mathtt{ee.act}[y]) = (\pi(x), \mathtt{cell}[x], 1) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment.events} $$

P1-0335【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{ee.first}[x] = [\mathtt{ee.act}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{ee.f}[x] = x],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment.events} $$

P1-0336【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{ee.e1}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{equipment.events} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{ee.act}[y]) = (x, 1) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0337【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{ee.is1}[x] = [\mathtt{ee.act}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{ee.e1}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big] = x],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment.events} $$

P1-0338【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{ee.b4}[x] = [\mathtt{ee.act}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{b4.u\_eq}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment.events} $$

P1-0339【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{ee.b3}[x] = [\mathtt{ee.first}[x] = 1] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{b3.a\_eq}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment.events} $$

P1-0340【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{ee.np}[x] = [\mathtt{ee.is1}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{np.e}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment.events} $$

P1-0341【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{ee.one}[x] = [\mathtt{ee.act}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment.events} $$

P1-0342【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{ee.listed}[x] = [\mathtt{replacement\_event\_list\#}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0343【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{be.cal}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} \;:\; (\mathtt{\_pos}[y]) = (\mathtt{cell}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{boundary.events} $$

P1-0344【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{be.b5}[x] = \mathtt{b5.u}^{\langle \mathtt{S}\rangle},\quad x \in \mathtt{boundary.events} $$

P1-0345【EVENTS】
$$ \mathtt{be.one}[x] = [\mathtt{b5.live}^{\langle \mathtt{S}\rangle} = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{boundary.events} $$


#### P1 / OPERATION

P1-0346【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x06.wfC}[x] = [\mathtt{grid.kind}[x] = \text{``CONSTANT''}] \cdot [\mathtt{grid.start}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0347【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x06.wfL}[x] = [\mathtt{grid.kind}[x] = \text{``LINEAR\_DECLINE\_TO\_FLOOR''}] \cdot [\mathtt{grid.start}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{grid.floor}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{grid.annual\_decline}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{grid.annual\_decline}[x] \le \infty] \cdot [\mathtt{grid.floor}[x] \le \mathtt{grid.start}[x]],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0348【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x06.pm1}[x] = \mathtt{\_pos}[x] \;+\; -1,\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.steps} $$

P1-0349【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x06.prev}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{operation.grid.steps} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{\_pos}[y]) = (\pi(x), \mathtt{x06.pm1}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.steps} $$

P1-0350【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x06.pp1}[x] = \mathtt{\_pos}[x] \;+\; 1,\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.steps} $$

P1-0351【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x06.next}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{operation.grid.steps} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{\_pos}[y]) = (\pi(x), \mathtt{x06.pp1}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.steps} $$

P1-0352【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x06.next\_from}[x] = \mathtt{from\_index}\big[\mathtt{x06.next}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.steps} $$

P1-0353【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x06.first0}[y] = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.steps} \,:\, \pi(x) = y} \Big([\mathtt{\_pos}[x] = 0] \cdot [\mathtt{from\_index}[x] = 0]\Big) $$

P1-0354【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x06.nbad}[y] = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.steps} \,:\, \pi(x) = y} \Big([\mathtt{x06.prev}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{from\_index}[x] \le \mathtt{from\_index}\big[\mathtt{x06.prev}[x]\big]]\Big) $$

P1-0355【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x06.wfS}[x] = [\mathtt{grid.kind}[x] = \text{``STEPWISE''}] \cdot [1 \le \mathtt{grid.steps\#}[x] \le \infty] \cdot [\mathtt{x06.first0}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{x06.nbad}[x] = 0],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0356【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x06.wf}[x] = [\mathtt{x06.wfC}[x] = 1] \;+\; [\mathtt{x06.wfL}[x] = 1] \;+\; [\mathtt{x06.wfS}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0357【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x06.sref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{operation.grid.steps} \;:\; (y) = (\mathtt{s}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table.xsteps} $$

P1-0358【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x06.sv}[y] = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table.xsteps} \,:\, \pi(x) = y} \Big([\mathtt{from\_index}\big[\mathtt{x06.sref}[x]\big] \le \mathtt{\_pos}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{x06.next\_from}\big[\mathtt{x06.sref}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{x06.sref}[x]\big] \;+\; [\mathtt{from\_index}\big[\mathtt{x06.sref}[x]\big] \le \mathtt{\_pos}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big]] \cdot [\mathtt{x06.next\_from}\big[\mathtt{x06.sref}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{x06.next\_from}\big[\mathtt{x06.sref}[x]\big] \le \mathtt{\_pos}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big]] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{x06.sref}[x]\big]\Big) $$

P1-0359【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x06.dl}[x] = [\mathtt{x06.wfL}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 1] \cdot \mathtt{grid.start}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \;+\; [\mathtt{x06.wfL}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 1] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{grid.annual\_decline}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{\_pos}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0360【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x06.g}[x] = [\mathtt{x06.wfC}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 1] \cdot \mathtt{grid.start}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \;+\; [\mathtt{x06.wfL}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{grid.floor}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \le \mathtt{x06.dl}[x]] \cdot \mathtt{x06.dl}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{x06.wfL}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 1] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{grid.floor}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \le \mathtt{x06.dl}[x]] \cdot \mathtt{grid.floor}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \;+\; [\mathtt{x06.wfS}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 1] \cdot \mathtt{x06.sv}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{x06.wf}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 0] \cdot \mathtt{value}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0361【OPERATION・CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{x06.m.av}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{x06.g}[x] \le \infty] \cdot \mathtt{x06.g}[x] \;+\; [-\infty \le \mathtt{x06.g}[x] < 0] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{x06.g}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0362【OPERATION・CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{x06.m.at}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{value}[x] \le \infty] \cdot \mathtt{value}[x] \;+\; [-\infty \le \mathtt{value}[x] < 0] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{value}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0363【OPERATION・CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{x06.m.mx}[x] = [\mathtt{x06.m.at}[x] \le \mathtt{x06.m.av}[x]] \cdot \mathtt{x06.m.av}[x] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{x06.m.at}[x] \le \mathtt{x06.m.av}[x]] \cdot \mathtt{x06.m.at}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0364【OPERATION・CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{x06.m.tol}[x] = 1e-12 \cdot \mathtt{x06.m.mx}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0365【OPERATION・CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{x06.m.d}[x] = [\mathtt{value}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{x06.g}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{value}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{value}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0366【OPERATION・CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{x06.m.nd}[x] = -1 \cdot \mathtt{x06.m.d}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0367【OPERATION・CLOSE】

P1-0370【OPERATION・PROFILE】

P1-0372【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】

P1-0373【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】

P1-0374【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】

P1-0381【OPERATION・PROFILE】
