$$ \mathtt{b64.cx.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\mathtt{onsite\_export.unit\~{}h}[x], \mathtt{electricity.unit\~{}h}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0407【OPERATION・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b64.cx.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{onsite\_export.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{electricity.unit\~{}h}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{onsite\_export.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{electricity.unit\~{}h}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{b64.cx.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{onsite\_export.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{electricity.unit\~{}h}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{b64.cx.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b64.cx.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0408【OPERATION・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b64.cx.f}[x] = [\mathtt{onsite\_export.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{electricity.unit\~{}h}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{onsite\_export.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{electricity.unit\~{}h}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{b64.cx.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{b64.cx.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{onsite\_export.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{electricity.unit\~{}h}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{b64.cx.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b64.cx.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{b64.cx.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0409【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{b64.computed}[x] = [\mathtt{electricity.unit\~{}t}[x] = \text{``year''}] \cdot [\mathtt{onsite\_generation.unit\~{}t}[x] = \text{``year''}] \cdot [\mathtt{onsite\_export.unit\~{}t}[x] = \text{``year''}] \cdot [\mathtt{b64.cg.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{b64.cx.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0410【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{b64.inner}[x] = [\mathtt{b64.computed}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 1] \cdot \mathtt{p.gen.v}[x] \cdot \mathtt{b64.cg.f}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \;+\; [\mathtt{b64.computed}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 1] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{p.exp.v}[x] \cdot \mathtt{b64.cx.f}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0411【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{b64.d}[x] = [\mathtt{b64.computed}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 1] \cdot \mathtt{p.el.v}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{b64.computed}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 1] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{b64.inner}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0412【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{b64.net}[x] = [\mathtt{b64.computed}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 1] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{b64.d}[x] \le \infty] \cdot \mathtt{b64.d}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0413【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{b64.mis}[x] = \mathtt{p.el.m.mis}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{p.gen.m.mis}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{p.exp.m.mis}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0414【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x06.gu\_ok}[x] = [\mathtt{grid.unit\~{}h}[x] = \text{``kgCO2e''}] \cdot [\mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x] = \text{``''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0415【OPERATION・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b61.c.xref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{\_none} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (x, \mathtt{electricity.unit\~{}h}[x], \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0416【OPERATION・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b61.c.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\mathtt{electricity.unit\~{}h}[x], \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0417【OPERATION・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b61.c.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{electricity.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{electricity.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{b61.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{electricity.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{b61.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b61.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0418【OPERATION・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b61.c.f}[x] = [\mathtt{electricity.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{electricity.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{b61.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{b61.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{electricity.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{b61.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b61.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{b61.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0419【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{b61.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{grid.licence\_status}[x] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{b64.computed}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{x06.gu\_ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{b61.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0420【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{b61.e}[x] = [\mathtt{b61.ok}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 1] \cdot \mathtt{b64.net}[x] \cdot \mathtt{b61.c.f}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{x06.g}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0421【OPERATION・PROFILE】
$$ \mathtt{p.gas.d}[x] = [\mathtt{gas.kind}[x] = \text{``CONSTANT''}] \cdot [\mathtt{gas.base}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; [\mathtt{gas.kind}[x] = \text{``LINEAR\_TREND''}] \cdot [\mathtt{gas.base}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{gas.slope}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0422【OPERATION・PROFILE】
$$ \mathtt{p.gas.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{operation.gas.table} \;:\; (\mathtt{\_pos}[y]) = (\mathtt{\_pos}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0423【OPERATION・PROFILE】
$$ \mathtt{p.gas.v}[x] = [\mathtt{gas.kind}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = \text{``CONSTANT''}] \cdot [\mathtt{gas.base}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{gas.base}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \;+\; [\mathtt{gas.kind}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = \text{``LINEAR\_TREND''}] \cdot [\mathtt{gas.base}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{gas.slope}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{gas.base}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \;+\; [\mathtt{gas.kind}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = \text{``LINEAR\_TREND''}] \cdot [\mathtt{gas.base}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{gas.slope}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{gas.slope}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{\_pos}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{p.gas.d}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 0] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{p.gas.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0424【OPERATION・PROFILE】
$$ \mathtt{p.gas.t}[x] = \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{p.gas.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0425【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.gas.m.av}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{p.gas.v}[x] \le \infty] \cdot \mathtt{p.gas.v}[x] \;+\; [-\infty \le \mathtt{p.gas.v}[x] < 0] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{p.gas.v}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0426【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.gas.m.at}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{p.gas.t}[x] \le \infty] \cdot \mathtt{p.gas.t}[x] \;+\; [-\infty \le \mathtt{p.gas.t}[x] < 0] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{p.gas.t}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0427【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.gas.m.mx}[x] = [\mathtt{p.gas.m.at}[x] \le \mathtt{p.gas.m.av}[x]] \cdot \mathtt{p.gas.m.av}[x] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{p.gas.m.at}[x] \le \mathtt{p.gas.m.av}[x]] \cdot \mathtt{p.gas.m.at}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0428【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.gas.m.tol}[x] = 1e-12 \cdot \mathtt{p.gas.m.mx}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0429【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.gas.m.d}[x] = [\mathtt{p.gas.t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{p.gas.v}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{p.gas.t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{p.gas.t}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0430【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.gas.m.nd}[x] = -1 \cdot \mathtt{p.gas.m.d}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0431【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.gas.m.mis}[x] = [\mathtt{p.gas.t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{p.gas.v}[x] = \mathtt{p.gas.t}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{p.gas.m.d}[x] \le \mathtt{p.gas.m.tol}[x]] \;+\; [\mathtt{p.gas.t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{p.gas.v}[x] = \mathtt{p.gas.t}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{p.gas.m.d}[x] \le \mathtt{p.gas.m.tol}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{p.gas.m.nd}[x] \le \mathtt{p.gas.m.tol}[x]],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0432【OPERATION・PROFILE】
$$ \mathtt{p.oil.d}[x] = [\mathtt{oil.kind}[x] = \text{``CONSTANT''}] \cdot [\mathtt{oil.base}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; [\mathtt{oil.kind}[x] = \text{``LINEAR\_TREND''}] \cdot [\mathtt{oil.base}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{oil.slope}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0433【OPERATION・PROFILE】
$$ \mathtt{p.oil.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{operation.oil.table} \;:\; (\mathtt{\_pos}[y]) = (\mathtt{\_pos}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0434【OPERATION・PROFILE】
$$ \mathtt{p.oil.v}[x] = [\mathtt{oil.kind}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = \text{``CONSTANT''}] \cdot [\mathtt{oil.base}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{oil.base}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \;+\; [\mathtt{oil.kind}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = \text{``LINEAR\_TREND''}] \cdot [\mathtt{oil.base}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{oil.slope}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{oil.base}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \;+\; [\mathtt{oil.kind}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = \text{``LINEAR\_TREND''}] \cdot [\mathtt{oil.base}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{oil.slope}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{oil.slope}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{\_pos}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{p.oil.d}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 0] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{p.oil.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0435【OPERATION・PROFILE】
$$ \mathtt{p.oil.t}[x] = \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{p.oil.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0436【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.oil.m.av}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{p.oil.v}[x] \le \infty] \cdot \mathtt{p.oil.v}[x] \;+\; [-\infty \le \mathtt{p.oil.v}[x] < 0] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{p.oil.v}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0437【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.oil.m.at}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{p.oil.t}[x] \le \infty] \cdot \mathtt{p.oil.t}[x] \;+\; [-\infty \le \mathtt{p.oil.t}[x] < 0] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{p.oil.t}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0438【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.oil.m.mx}[x] = [\mathtt{p.oil.m.at}[x] \le \mathtt{p.oil.m.av}[x]] \cdot \mathtt{p.oil.m.av}[x] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{p.oil.m.at}[x] \le \mathtt{p.oil.m.av}[x]] \cdot \mathtt{p.oil.m.at}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0439【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.oil.m.tol}[x] = 1e-12 \cdot \mathtt{p.oil.m.mx}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0440【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.oil.m.d}[x] = [\mathtt{p.oil.t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{p.oil.v}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{p.oil.t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{p.oil.t}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0441【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.oil.m.nd}[x] = -1 \cdot \mathtt{p.oil.m.d}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0442【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.oil.m.mis}[x] = [\mathtt{p.oil.t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{p.oil.v}[x] = \mathtt{p.oil.t}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{p.oil.m.d}[x] \le \mathtt{p.oil.m.tol}[x]] \;+\; [\mathtt{p.oil.t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{p.oil.v}[x] = \mathtt{p.oil.t}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{p.oil.m.d}[x] \le \mathtt{p.oil.m.tol}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{p.oil.m.nd}[x] \le \mathtt{p.oil.m.tol}[x]],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0443【OPERATION・PROFILE】
$$ \mathtt{p.dh.d}[x] = [\mathtt{district\_heat.kind}[x] = \text{``CONSTANT''}] \cdot [\mathtt{district\_heat.base}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; [\mathtt{district\_heat.kind}[x] = \text{``LINEAR\_TREND''}] \cdot [\mathtt{district\_heat.base}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{district\_heat.slope}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0444【OPERATION・PROFILE】
$$ \mathtt{p.dh.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{operation.district\_heat.table} \;:\; (\mathtt{\_pos}[y]) = (\mathtt{\_pos}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0445【OPERATION・PROFILE】
$$ \mathtt{p.dh.v}[x] = [\mathtt{district\_heat.kind}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = \text{``CONSTANT''}] \cdot [\mathtt{district\_heat.base}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{district\_heat.base}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \;+\; [\mathtt{district\_heat.kind}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = \text{``LINEAR\_TREND''}] \cdot [\mathtt{district\_heat.base}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{district\_heat.slope}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{district\_heat.base}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \;+\; [\mathtt{district\_heat.kind}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = \text{``LINEAR\_TREND''}] \cdot [\mathtt{district\_heat.base}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{district\_heat.slope}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{district\_heat.slope}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{\_pos}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{p.dh.d}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 0] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{p.dh.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0446【OPERATION・PROFILE】
$$ \mathtt{p.dh.t}[x] = \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{p.dh.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0447【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.dh.m.av}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{p.dh.v}[x] \le \infty] \cdot \mathtt{p.dh.v}[x] \;+\; [-\infty \le \mathtt{p.dh.v}[x] < 0] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{p.dh.v}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0448【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.dh.m.at}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{p.dh.t}[x] \le \infty] \cdot \mathtt{p.dh.t}[x] \;+\; [-\infty \le \mathtt{p.dh.t}[x] < 0] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{p.dh.t}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0449【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.dh.m.mx}[x] = [\mathtt{p.dh.m.at}[x] \le \mathtt{p.dh.m.av}[x]] \cdot \mathtt{p.dh.m.av}[x] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{p.dh.m.at}[x] \le \mathtt{p.dh.m.av}[x]] \cdot \mathtt{p.dh.m.at}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0450【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.dh.m.tol}[x] = 1e-12 \cdot \mathtt{p.dh.m.mx}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0451【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.dh.m.d}[x] = [\mathtt{p.dh.t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{p.dh.v}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{p.dh.t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{p.dh.t}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0452【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.dh.m.nd}[x] = -1 \cdot \mathtt{p.dh.m.d}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0453【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.dh.m.mis}[x] = [\mathtt{p.dh.t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{p.dh.v}[x] = \mathtt{p.dh.t}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{p.dh.m.d}[x] \le \mathtt{p.dh.m.tol}[x]] \;+\; [\mathtt{p.dh.t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{p.dh.v}[x] = \mathtt{p.dh.t}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{p.dh.m.d}[x] \le \mathtt{p.dh.m.tol}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{p.dh.m.nd}[x] \le \mathtt{p.dh.m.tol}[x]],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0454【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{b62.gas.f.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{factors} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{gas\_factor\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0455【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{b62.gas.f.unit}[x] = \mathtt{unit}\big[\mathtt{b62.gas.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0456【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b62.gas.f.c.xref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{\_none} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (x, \mathtt{gas.unit\~{}h}[x], \mathtt{b62.gas.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0457【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b62.gas.f.c.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\mathtt{gas.unit\~{}h}[x], \mathtt{b62.gas.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0458【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b62.gas.f.c.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{gas.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b62.gas.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{gas.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b62.gas.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{b62.gas.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{gas.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b62.gas.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{b62.gas.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b62.gas.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0459【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b62.gas.f.c.f}[x] = [\mathtt{gas.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b62.gas.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{gas.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b62.gas.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{b62.gas.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{b62.gas.f.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{gas.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b62.gas.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{b62.gas.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b62.gas.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{b62.gas.f.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0460【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{b62.gas.f.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{gas.unit\~{}h}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{b62.gas.f.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.B6.is}\big[\mathtt{b62.gas.f.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{b62.gas.f.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0461【OPERATION・ANNUALF】
$$ \mathtt{b62.gas.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{b62.gas.f.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{gas.unit\~{}t}[x] = \text{``year''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0462【OPERATION・ANNUALF】
$$ \mathtt{b62.gas.fv}[x] = [\mathtt{b62.gas.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{b62.gas.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0463【OPERATION・ANNUALF】
$$ \mathtt{b62.gas.act}[x] = [\mathtt{b62.gas.ok}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 1] \cdot \mathtt{p.gas.v}[x] \cdot \mathtt{b62.gas.f.c.f}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0464【OPERATION・ANNUALF】
$$ \mathtt{b62.gas.e}[x] = [\mathtt{b62.gas.ok}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 1] \cdot \mathtt{p.gas.v}[x] \cdot \mathtt{b62.gas.f.c.f}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{b62.gas.fv}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0465【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{b62.oil.f.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{factors} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{oil\_factor\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0466【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR】

P1-0467【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】

P1-0468【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】

P1-0469【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】

P1-0470【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】

P1-0472【OPERATION・ANNUALF】

P1-0473【OPERATION・ANNUALF】

P1-0474【OPERATION・ANNUALF】

P1-0475【OPERATION・ANNUALF】

P1-0481【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】

P1-0482【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{b62.dh.f.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{district\_heat.unit\~{}h}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{b62.dh.f.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.B6.is}\big[\mathtt{b62.dh.f.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{b62.dh.f.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0488【OPERATION】

P1-0489【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{b62.mis}[x] = \mathtt{p.gas.m.mis}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{p.oil.m.mis}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{p.dh.m.mis}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0490【OPERATION】

P1-0491【OPERATION】

P1-0492【OPERATION】

P1-0493【OPERATION】

P1-0494【OPERATION・PROFILE】
$$ \mathtt{p.wat.d}[x] = [\mathtt{water.kind}[x] = \text{``CONSTANT''}] \cdot [\mathtt{water.base}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; [\mathtt{water.kind}[x] = \text{``LINEAR\_TREND''}] \cdot [\mathtt{water.base}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{water.slope}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0496【OPERATION・PROFILE】
$$ \mathtt{p.wat.v}[x] = [\mathtt{water.kind}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} = \text{``CONSTANT''}] \cdot [\mathtt{water.base}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{water.base}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} \;+\; [\mathtt{water.kind}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} = \text{``LINEAR\_TREND''}] \cdot [\mathtt{water.base}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{water.slope}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{water.base}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} \;+\; [\mathtt{water.kind}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} = \text{``LINEAR\_TREND''}] \cdot [\mathtt{water.base}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{water.slope}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{water.slope}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{\_pos}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{p.wat.d}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} = 0] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{p.wat.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0497【OPERATION・PROFILE】
$$ \mathtt{p.wat.t}[x] = \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{p.wat.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0498【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.wat.m.av}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{p.wat.v}[x] \le \infty] \cdot \mathtt{p.wat.v}[x] \;+\; [-\infty \le \mathtt{p.wat.v}[x] < 0] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{p.wat.v}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0499【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.wat.m.at}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{p.wat.t}[x] \le \infty] \cdot \mathtt{p.wat.t}[x] \;+\; [-\infty \le \mathtt{p.wat.t}[x] < 0] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{p.wat.t}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0500【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】

P1-0501【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】

P1-0502【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.wat.m.d}[x] = [\mathtt{p.wat.t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{p.wat.v}[x] \;+\; [\mathtt{p.wat.t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot -1 \cdot \mathtt{p.wat.t}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0503【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】

P1-0504【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】

P1-0513【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】

P1-0514【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】

P1-0519【OPERATION・PROFILE】

P1-0525【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.msw.m.nd}[x] = -1 \cdot \mathtt{p.msw.m.d}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0526【OPERATION・PROFILE/CLOSE】
$$ \mathtt{p.msw.m.mis}[x] = [\mathtt{p.msw.t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{p.msw.v}[x] = \mathtt{p.msw.t}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{p.msw.m.d}[x] \le \mathtt{p.msw.m.tol}[x]] \;+\; [\mathtt{p.msw.t}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{p.msw.v}[x] = \mathtt{p.msw.t}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{p.msw.m.d}[x] \le \mathtt{p.msw.m.tol}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{p.msw.m.nd}[x] \le \mathtt{p.msw.m.tol}[x]],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0527【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{b71.f.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{factors} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{water\_factor\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0528【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{b71.f.unit}[x] = \mathtt{unit}\big[\mathtt{b71.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0529【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b71.f.c.xref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{\_none} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (x, \mathtt{water.unit\~{}h}[x], \mathtt{b71.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0530【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b71.f.c.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\mathtt{water.unit\~{}h}[x], \mathtt{b71.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0531【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b71.f.c.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{water.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b71.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{water.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b71.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{b71.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{water.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b71.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{b71.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b71.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0532【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b71.f.c.f}[x] = [\mathtt{water.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b71.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{water.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b71.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{b71.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{b71.f.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{water.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b71.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{b71.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b71.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{b71.f.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0533【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{b71.f.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{water.unit\~{}h}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{b71.f.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.B7.is}\big[\mathtt{b71.f.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{b71.f.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0534【OPERATION・ANNUALF】
$$ \mathtt{b71.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{b71.f.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{water.unit\~{}t}[x] = \text{``year''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0535【OPERATION・ANNUALF】
$$ \mathtt{b71.fv}[x] = [\mathtt{b71.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{b71.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0536【OPERATION・ANNUALF】
$$ \mathtt{b71.act}[x] = [\mathtt{b71.ok}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} = 1] \cdot \mathtt{p.wat.v}[x] \cdot \mathtt{b71.f.c.f}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0537【OPERATION・ANNUALF】
$$ \mathtt{b71.e}[x] = [\mathtt{b71.ok}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} = 1] \cdot \mathtt{p.wat.v}[x] \cdot \mathtt{b71.f.c.f}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{b71.fv}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0538【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{b72.f.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{factors} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{sewage\_factor\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0539【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{b72.f.unit}[x] = \mathtt{unit}\big[\mathtt{b72.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0540【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b72.f.c.xref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{\_none} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (x, \mathtt{sewage.unit\~{}h}[x], \mathtt{b72.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0541【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b72.f.c.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\mathtt{sewage.unit\~{}h}[x], \mathtt{b72.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0542【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b72.f.c.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{sewage.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b72.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{sewage.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b72.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{b72.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{sewage.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b72.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{b72.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b72.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0543【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b72.f.c.f}[x] = [\mathtt{sewage.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b72.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{sewage.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b72.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{b72.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{b72.f.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{sewage.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b72.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{b72.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b72.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{b72.f.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0544【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{b72.f.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{sewage.unit\~{}h}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{b72.f.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.B7.is}\big[\mathtt{b72.f.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{b72.f.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0545【OPERATION・ANNUALF】
$$ \mathtt{b72.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{b72.f.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{sewage.unit\~{}t}[x] = \text{``year''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0546【OPERATION・ANNUALF】
$$ \mathtt{b72.fv}[x] = [\mathtt{b72.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{b72.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0547【OPERATION・ANNUALF】
$$ \mathtt{b72.act}[x] = [\mathtt{b72.ok}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} = 1] \cdot \mathtt{p.sew.v}[x] \cdot \mathtt{b72.f.c.f}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0548【OPERATION・ANNUALF】
$$ \mathtt{b72.e}[x] = [\mathtt{b72.ok}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} = 1] \cdot \mathtt{p.sew.v}[x] \cdot \mathtt{b72.f.c.f}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{b72.fv}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0549【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{b73.f.ref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{factors} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{waste\_factor\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0550【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{b73.f.unit}[x] = \mathtt{unit}\big[\mathtt{b73.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0551【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b73.f.c.xref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{\_none} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (x, \mathtt{municipal\_waste.unit\~{}h}[x], \mathtt{b73.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0552【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b73.f.c.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\mathtt{municipal\_waste.unit\~{}h}[x], \mathtt{b73.f.unit}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0553【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b73.f.c.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{municipal\_waste.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b73.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{municipal\_waste.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b73.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{b73.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{municipal\_waste.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b73.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{b73.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b73.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0554【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR/CONV】
$$ \mathtt{b73.f.c.f}[x] = [\mathtt{municipal\_waste.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b73.f.unit}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{municipal\_waste.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b73.f.unit}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{b73.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{b73.f.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{municipal\_waste.unit\~{}h}[x] = \mathtt{b73.f.unit}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{b73.f.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b73.f.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{b73.f.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0555【OPERATION・ANNUALF/FACTOR】
$$ \mathtt{b73.f.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{municipal\_waste.unit\~{}h}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{licence\_status}\big[\mathtt{b73.f.ref}[x]\big] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}] \cdot [\mathtt{stage.B7.is}\big[\mathtt{b73.f.ref}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{b73.f.c.ok}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0556【OPERATION・ANNUALF】
$$ \mathtt{b73.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{b73.f.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{municipal\_waste.unit\~{}t}[x] = \text{``year''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0557【OPERATION・ANNUALF】
$$ \mathtt{b73.fv}[x] = [\mathtt{b73.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{b73.f.ref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0558【OPERATION・ANNUALF】
$$ \mathtt{b73.act}[x] = [\mathtt{b73.ok}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} = 1] \cdot \mathtt{p.msw.v}[x] \cdot \mathtt{b73.f.c.f}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0559【OPERATION・ANNUALF】
$$ \mathtt{b73.e}[x] = [\mathtt{b73.ok}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} = 1] \cdot \mathtt{p.msw.v}[x] \cdot \mathtt{b73.f.c.f}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{b73.fv}^{\langle \mathtt{water\_waste}\rangle},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0560【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{b74.a}[x] = \mathtt{b71.e}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{b72.e}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{b73.e}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

P1-0561【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{b71.nsk}[x] = [\mathtt{b71.ok}[x] = 0],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0562【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{b72.nsk}[x] = [\mathtt{b72.ok}[x] = 0],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0563【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{b73.nsk}[x] = [\mathtt{b73.ok}[x] = 0],\quad x \in \mathtt{water\_waste} $$

P1-0564【OPERATION・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x11.c.xref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{\_none} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (x, \text{``kWh''}, \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0565【OPERATION・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x11.c.cref}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{unit\_conversions} \;:\; (\mathtt{from\_unit}[y], \mathtt{to\_unit}[y]) = (\text{``kWh''}, \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0566【OPERATION・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x11.c.ok}[x] = [\text{``kWh''} = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\text{``kWh''} = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{x11.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\text{``kWh''} = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{x11.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{x11.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0567【OPERATION・CONV】
$$ \mathtt{x11.c.f}[x] = [\text{``kWh''} = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \;+\; \neg[\text{``kWh''} = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \cdot [\mathtt{x11.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{x11.c.xref}[x]\big] \;+\; \neg[\text{``kWh''} = \mathtt{grid.unit\~{}t}[x]] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{x11.c.xref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{x11.c.cref}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \mathtt{factor}\big[\mathtt{x11.c.cref}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0568【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x11.ok}[x] = [0 < \mathtt{gross\_floor\_area}^{\langle \mathtt{project}\rangle} < \infty] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{electricity\_per\_m2\_per\_year}[x] < \infty] \cdot [\mathtt{x06.gu\_ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{x11.c.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{grid.licence\_status}[x] = \text{``PERMITTED\_PUBLIC''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation} $$

P1-0569【OPERATION】
$$ \mathtt{x11.a}[x] = [\mathtt{x11.ok}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} = 1] \cdot \mathtt{gross\_floor\_area}^{\langle \mathtt{project}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{electricity\_per\_m2\_per\_year}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{x11.c.f}^{\langle \mathtt{operation}\rangle} \cdot \mathtt{x06.g}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$


#### P1 / REFRIGERANT

P1-0570【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b1.ok}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{charge\_mass\_kg}[x] < \infty] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{gwp}[x] < \infty] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{annual\_leak\_rate}[x] \le 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants} $$

P1-0571【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b1.q}[x] = [\mathtt{b1.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{top\_up}[x] = 1] \;+\; [\mathtt{b1.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{top\_up}[x] = 0] \cdot (1-\mathtt{annual\_leak\_rate}[x]),\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants} $$

P1-0572【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b1.lok}[x] = [\mathtt{b1.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{replacement\_loss\_rate}[x] \le 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants} $$

P1-0573【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b1.rok}[x] = [\mathtt{b1.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{end\_of\_life\_recovery\_rate}[x] \le 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants} $$

P1-0574【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b1.k4}[x] = [\mathtt{b1.rok}[x] = 1] \cdot (1-\mathtt{end\_of\_life\_recovery\_rate}[x]) \cdot \mathtt{gwp}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants} $$

P1-0575【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b1.k4m0}[x] = [\mathtt{b1.rok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{b1.k4}[x] \cdot \mathtt{charge\_mass\_kg}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants} $$

P1-0576【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b1.l3}[x] = [\mathtt{b1.lok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{replacement\_loss\_rate}[x] \cdot \mathtt{gwp}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants} $$

P1-0577【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b11.ok}[x] = [0 \le \mathtt{charge\_mass\_kg}[x] < \infty] \cdot [0 \le \mathtt{gwp}[x] < \infty],\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants} $$

P1-0578【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b11.v} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{refrigerants}} \Big([\mathtt{b11.ok}[x] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{charge\_mass\_kg}[x] \cdot \mathtt{gwp}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0579【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b11.nsk} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{refrigerants}} \Big([\mathtt{b11.ok}[x] = 0]\Big) $$

P1-0580【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b12.nsk} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{refrigerants}} \Big([\mathtt{b1.ok}[x] = 0]\Big) $$

P1-0581【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b13.nsk} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{refrigerants}} \Big([\mathtt{b1.lok}[x] = 0]\Big) $$

P1-0582【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b14.nsk} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{refrigerants}} \Big([\mathtt{b1.rok}[x] = 0]\Big) $$

P1-0583【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b1.act}[x] = [\mathtt{src}[x] = \text{``LIST''}] \cdot [\mathtt{change\_years\#}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \;+\; [\mathtt{src}[x] = \text{``CYCLE''}] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{change\_years\#}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants.events} $$

P1-0584【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{re.cal}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} \;:\; (\mathtt{\_pos}[y]) = (\mathtt{cell}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants.events} $$

P1-0585【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{dy.cal}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} \;:\; (\mathtt{\_pos}[y]) = (\mathtt{k}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants.years} $$

P1-0586【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b1.ev1}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{refrigerants.events} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{cell}[y], \mathtt{b1.act}[y]) = (\pi(x), \mathtt{k}[x], 1) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants.years} $$

P1-0587【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b1.p}[x] = [\mathtt{b1.ev1}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants.years} $$

P1-0588【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b1.km1}[x] = \mathtt{k}[x] \;+\; -1,\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants.years} $$

P1-0589【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b1.kp1}[x] = \mathtt{k}[x] \;+\; 1,\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants.years} $$

P1-0590【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b1.pv1}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{refrigerants.years} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{k}[y]) = (\pi(x), \mathtt{b1.km1}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants.years} $$

P1-0591【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b1.c0}[x] = [\mathtt{b1.ok}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{b1.pv1}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b1.p}\big[\mathtt{b1.pv1}[x]\big] = 0] \cdot \mathtt{b1.q}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants.years} $$

P1-0592【REFRIGERANT】
$$ \mathtt{b1.u0}[x] = [\mathtt{b1.ok}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{k}[x] = 0] \cdot \mathtt{charge\_mass\_kg}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big] \;+\; [\mathtt{b1.ok}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{b1.pv1}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{b1.p}\big[\mathtt{b1.pv1}[x]\big] = 1] \cdot \mathtt{charge\_mass\_kg}\big[\mathtt{\_parent}[x]\big],\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants.years} $$

P1-0593【REFRIGERANT・DOUBLE】
$$ \mathtt{b1.dbl0.ks}[x] = \mathtt{k}[x] \;+\; -1,\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants.years} $$

P1-0594【REFRIGERANT・DOUBLE】

#### P1 / SYS-01

P1-0746【SYS-01】
$$ \mathtt{s1.app\_sl}[x] = [\mathtt{service\_life}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{service\_life}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{replacement\_event\_list\#}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment} $$

P1-0747【SYS-01】
$$ \mathtt{s1.app\_nl}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{service\_life}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{change\_years\#}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants} $$

P1-0748【SYS-01】
$$ \mathtt{s1.one.project}[x] = 1,\quad x \in \mathtt{project} $$

P1-0749【SYS-01】
$$ \mathtt{s1.prov.project}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{provenance} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{provenance\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{project} $$

P1-0750【SYS-01】
$$ \mathtt{s1.o.p.area}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{project.states} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{key}[y]) = (x, \text{``gross\_floor\_area''}) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{project} $$

P1-0751【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.p.area.d.OBSERVED}[x] = [\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.p.area}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.p.area}[x]\big] = \text{``OBSERVED''}] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.p.area}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{s1.prov.project}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{value\_state}\big[\mathtt{s1.prov.project}[x]\big] = \text{``OBSERVED''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{project} $$

P1-0752【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.p.area.d.ESTIMATE}[x] = [\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.p.area}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.p.area}[x]\big] = \text{``ESTIMATE''}] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.p.area}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{s1.prov.project}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{value\_state}\big[\mathtt{s1.prov.project}[x]\big] = \text{``ESTIMATE''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{project} $$

P1-0753【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.p.area.d.EXPLICIT\_ZERO}[x] = [\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.p.area}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.p.area}[x]\big] = \text{``EXPLICIT\_ZERO''}] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.p.area}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{s1.prov.project}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{value\_state}\big[\mathtt{s1.prov.project}[x]\big] = \text{``EXPLICIT\_ZERO''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{project} $$

P1-0754【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.p.area.d.MISSING}[x] = [\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.p.area}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.p.area}[x]\big] = \text{``MISSING''}] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.p.area}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{s1.prov.project}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{value\_state}\big[\mathtt{s1.prov.project}[x]\big] = \text{``MISSING''}] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.p.area}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{s1.prov.project}[x] \neq \bot],\quad x \in \mathtt{project} $$

P1-0755【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.p.area.d.DERIVED}[x] = [\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.p.area}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.p.area}[x]\big] = \text{``DERIVED''}] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.p.area}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{s1.prov.project}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{value\_state}\big[\mathtt{s1.prov.project}[x]\big] = \text{``DERIVED''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{project} $$

P1-0756【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.p.area.live}[x] = [\mathtt{s1.one.project}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{gross\_floor\_area}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{s1.p.area.d.DERIVED}[x] = 0] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{gross\_floor\_area}[x] = 0],\quad x \in \mathtt{project} $$

P1-0757【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.p.area.s.MISSING}[x] = [\mathtt{s1.one.project}[x] = 1] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{gross\_floor\_area}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; [\mathtt{s1.p.area.live}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{s1.p.area.d.MISSING}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{project} $$

P1-0758【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.p.area.s.DERIVED}[x] = [\mathtt{s1.one.project}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{gross\_floor\_area}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{s1.p.area.d.DERIVED}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{project} $$

P1-0759【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.p.area.s.EXPLICIT\_ZERO}[x] = [\mathtt{s1.one.project}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{gross\_floor\_area}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{s1.p.area.d.DERIVED}[x] = 0] \cdot [\mathtt{gross\_floor\_area}[x] = 0] \;+\; [\mathtt{s1.p.area.live}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{s1.p.area.d.EXPLICIT\_ZERO}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{project} $$

P1-0760【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.p.area.s.OBSERVED}[x] = [\mathtt{s1.p.area.live}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{s1.p.area.d.OBSERVED}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{project} $$

P1-0761【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.p.area.s.ESTIMATE}[x] = [\mathtt{s1.p.area.live}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{s1.p.area.d.ESTIMATE}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{project} $$

P1-0762【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.p.area.n.OBSERVED} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{project}} \Big(\mathtt{s1.p.area.s.OBSERVED}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0763【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.p.area.n.ESTIMATE} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{project}} \Big(\mathtt{s1.p.area.s.ESTIMATE}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0764【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.p.area.n.EXPLICIT\_ZERO} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{project}} \Big(\mathtt{s1.p.area.s.EXPLICIT\_ZERO}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0765【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.p.area.n.MISSING} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{project}} \Big(\mathtt{s1.p.area.s.MISSING}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0766【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.p.area.n.DERIVED} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{project}} \Big(\mathtt{s1.p.area.s.DERIVED}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0767【SYS-01】
$$ \mathtt{s1.one.materials}[x] = 1,\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0768【SYS-01】
$$ \mathtt{s1.prov.materials}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{provenance} \;:\; (\mathtt{key}[y]) = (\mathtt{provenance\_id}[x]) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0769【SYS-01】
$$ \mathtt{s1.o.m.quantity}[x] = \min\left\{ y \in \mathtt{materials.states} \;:\; (\pi(y), \mathtt{key}[y]) = (x, \text{``quantity''}) \right\},\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0770【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.m.quantity.d.OBSERVED}[x] = [\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.m.quantity}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.m.quantity}[x]\big] = \text{``OBSERVED''}] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.m.quantity}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{s1.prov.materials}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{value\_state}\big[\mathtt{s1.prov.materials}[x]\big] = \text{``OBSERVED''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0771【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.m.quantity.d.ESTIMATE}[x] = [\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.m.quantity}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.m.quantity}[x]\big] = \text{``ESTIMATE''}] \;+\; \neg[\mathtt{value}\big[\mathtt{s1.o.m.quantity}[x]\big] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{s1.prov.materials}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{value\_state}\big[\mathtt{s1.prov.materials}[x]\big] = \text{``ESTIMATE''}],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0776【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.m.quantity.s.MISSING}[x] = [\mathtt{s1.one.materials}[x] = 1] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{quantity}[x] \neq \bot] \;+\; [\mathtt{s1.m.quantity.live}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{s1.m.quantity.d.MISSING}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0777【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.m.quantity.s.DERIVED}[x] = [\mathtt{s1.one.materials}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{quantity}[x] \neq \bot] \cdot [\mathtt{s1.m.quantity.d.DERIVED}[x] = 1],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials} $$

P1-0779【SYS-01・STATE】

P1-0780【SYS-01・STATE】

P1-0781【SYS-01・STATE】

P1-0782【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.m.quantity.n.ESTIMATE} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big(\mathtt{s1.m.quantity.s.ESTIMATE}[x]\Big) $$

P1-0783【SYS-01・STATE】
$$ \mathtt{s1.m.quantity.n.EXPLICIT\_ZERO} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big(\mathtt{s1.m.quantity.s.EXPLICIT\_ZERO}[x]\Big) $$

$$ \mathtt{s1.m.quantity.n.MISSING} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials}} \Big(\mathtt{s1.m.quantity.s.MISSING}[x]\Big) $$

$$ \mathtt{s2.sm}[x] = \mathtt{s2.sm.m.gen}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.m.part}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.m.dlv}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.m.tr}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.m.inst}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.m.haul}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.m.rt3}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.m.rt4}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.e.fac}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.e.dlv}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.e.ins}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.e.eol}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.c.fuel}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.c.waste}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.o.gas}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.o.oil}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.o.dh}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.w.wat}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.w.sew}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.w.msw}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.d.dem}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.d.haul}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.q.cu}[x] \;+\; \mathtt{s2.sm.x.cost}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{factors} $$

P1-1345【SYS-02】
$$ \mathtt{sys2.nsm} = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{factors}} \Big([1 \le \mathtt{s2.sm}[x] \le \infty]\Big) $$

P1-1346【SYS-02】
$$ \mathtt{sys2.ok}[x] = [\mathtt{sys2.nbad}[x] = 0] \cdot [\mathtt{sys2.nsm}[x] = 0],\quad x \in \mathtt{S} $$

### 3.2 TIME：積和②a（評価期間 T での節点表）（95 ステップ：積和 95、検索 0）


#### TIME / TIME

TIME-0001【TIME】
$$ \mathtt{Tin}[x] = \mathtt{T}[x] \cdot 0.999999999,\quad x \in \mathtt{TT} $$

TIME-0002【TIME】
$$ \mathtt{t.in}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{T}^{\langle \mathtt{TT}\rangle} \le \mathtt{\_pos}[x]],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

TIME-0003【TIME】
$$ \mathtt{t.dT}[x] = \mathtt{T}^{\langle \mathtt{TT}\rangle} \;+\; -1 \cdot \mathtt{\_pos}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

TIME-0004【TIME】
$$ \mathtt{t.w}[x] = [\mathtt{t.in}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{cal.kp1}[x] \le \mathtt{T}^{\langle \mathtt{TT}\rangle}] \;+\; [\mathtt{t.in}[x] = 1] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{cal.kp1}[x] \le \mathtt{T}^{\langle \mathtt{TT}\rangle}] \cdot \mathtt{t.dT}[x],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

TIME-0005【TIME】
$$ \mathtt{t.last}[x] = [\mathtt{t.in}[x] = 1] \cdot [\mathtt{T}^{\langle \mathtt{TT}\rangle} \le \mathtt{cal.kp1}[x]],\quad x \in \mathtt{operation.grid.table} $$

TIME-0006【TIME】
$$ \mathtt{t.in\_m}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{Tin}^{\langle \mathtt{TT}\rangle} \le \mathtt{tau}[x]],\quad x \in \mathtt{materials.events} $$

TIME-0007【TIME】
$$ \mathtt{t.in\_e}[x] = [\mathtt{ee.act}[x] = 1] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{Tin}^{\langle \mathtt{TT}\rangle} \le \mathtt{tau}[x]],\quad x \in \mathtt{equipment.events} $$

TIME-0008【TIME】
$$ \mathtt{t.in\_r}[x] = [\mathtt{b1.act}[x] = 1] \cdot \neg[\mathtt{Tin}^{\langle \mathtt{TT}\rangle} \le \mathtt{tau}[x]],\quad x \in \mathtt{refrigerants.events} $$

TIME-0009【TIME】
$$ \mathtt{t.in\_b}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{Tin}^{\langle \mathtt{TT}\rangle} \le \mathtt{tau}[x]],\quad x \in \mathtt{boundary.events} $$

TIME-0010【TIME】
$$ \mathtt{t.in\_c}[x] = \neg[\mathtt{Tin}^{\langle \mathtt{TT}\rangle} \le \mathtt{tau}[x]],\quad x \in \mathtt{CYC.events} $$

TIME-0011【TIME】
$$ \mathtt{t.cnt\_m}[y] = \sum_{x \in \mathtt{materials.events} \,:\, \pi(x) = y} \Big(\mathtt{t.in\_m}[x]\Big) $$

TIME-0012【TIME】

TIME-0013【TIME】

TIME-0016【TIME】

TIME-0017【TIME】

TIME-0023【TIME】

TIME-0031【TIME】
